## Analytics

*Analytics platforms, SDK's, error tracking and real-time answers about your app*

* [Segment](https://github.com/segmentio/analytics-ios) ⭐ 418 | 🐛 45 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2025-03-27 - The hassle-free way to integrate analytics into any iOS application.
* [Matomo](https://github.com/matomo-org/matomo-sdk-ios) ⭐ 402 | 🐛 21 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-04-08 - The MatomoTracker is an iOS, tvOS and macOS SDK for sending app analytics to a Matomo server.
* [Answers by Fabric](https://get.fabric.io) - Answers gives you real-time insight into people’s experience in your app.
* [Aptabase](https://aptabase.com/for-swift) - Open Source, Privacy-First and Simple Analytics for Swift Apps.
* [Bugsnag](https://www.bugsnag.com/platforms/ios-crash-reporting) - Error tracking with a free tier. Error reports include data on device, release, user, and allows arbitrary data.
* [Countly](https://count.ly) - Open source, mobile & web analytics, crash reports and push notifications platform for iOS & Android.
* [devtodev](https://www.devtodev.com/) - Comprehensive analytics service that improves your project and saves time for product development.
* [Embrace](http://embrace.io) - Mobile observability, built on OpenTelemetry, to provide user-focused, reliable apps.
* [Emerge Tools](https://www.emergetools.com) - Prevent app size & performance regressions on every pull request, get automated insights on how to improve.
* [Instabug](https://instabug.com) - In-app feedback, Bug and Crash reporting, Fix Bugs Faster through user-steps, video recordings, screen annotation, network requests logging.
* [Measure](https://measure.sh/) - Open source, self-hostable mobile app monitoring with error tracking, performance tracing and full session timelines to spend less time stitching context and fix issues faster.
* [Mixpanel](https://mixpanel.com/) - Advanced analytics platform.
* [MOCA Analytics](https://www.mocaplatform.com/features) - Paid cross-platform analytics backend.
* [Sentry](https://sentry.io/) - Sentry provides self-hosted and cloud-based error monitoring that helps all software teams discover, triage, and prioritize errors in real-time.
* [Shake](https://www.shakebugs.com/) - In-app feedback and bug reporting tool. Fix app bugs up to 50x faster with detailed device data, repro steps, video recording, black box data, network requests and custom logging.

**[back to top](#contributing-and-collaborating)**

## App Routing

*Elegant URL routing, navigation frameworks, deep links and more*

* [JLRoutes](https://github.com/joeldev/JLRoutes) ⭐ 5,703 | 🐛 12 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2023-02-21 - URL routing library for iOS with a simple block-based API.
* [DeepLinkKit](https://github.com/button/DeepLinkKit) ⭐ 3,453 | 🐛 22 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2025-11-25 - A splendid route-matching, block-based way to handle your deep links.
* [URLNavigator](https://github.com/devxoul/URLNavigator) ⭐ 3,346 | 🐛 45 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2024-05-24 - Elegant URL Routing for Swift
* [RxFlow](https://github.com/RxSwiftCommunity/RxFlow) ⭐ 1,911 | 🐛 2 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2025-05-21 - Navigation framework for iOS applications based on a Reactive Flow Coordinator pattern.
* [Appz](https://github.com/SwiftKitz/Appz) ⭐ 1,177 | 🐛 7 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2021-09-16 - Easily launch and deeplink into external applications, falling back to web if not installed.
* [RouteComposer](https://github.com/ekazaev/route-composer) ⭐ 929 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-08-18 - Library that helps to handle view controllers composition, routing and deeplinking tasks.
* [ApplicationCoordinator](https://github.com/AndreyPanov/ApplicationCoordinator) ⭐ 848 | 🐛 6 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2022-10-28 - Coordinator is an object that handles navigation flow and shares flow’s handling for the next coordinator after switching on the next chain.
* [Composable Navigator](https://github.com/Bahn-X/swift-composable-navigator) ⭐ 589 | 🐛 11 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2022-02-18 - An open source library for building deep-linkable SwiftUI applications with composition, testing and ergonomics in mind
* [WAAppRouting](https://github.com/Wasappli/WAAppRouting) ⭐ 587 | 🐛 4 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2016-05-19 - iOS routing done right. Handles both URL recognition and controller displaying with parsed parameters. All in one line, controller stack preserved automatically!
* [Crossroad](https://github.com/giginet/Crossroad) ⭐ 427 | 🐛 3 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2024-02-19 - Crossroad is an URL router focused on handling Custom URL Schemes. Using this, you can route multiple URL schemes and fetch arguments and parameters easily.
* [SwiftCurrent](https://github.com/wwt/SwiftCurrent) ⭐ 305 | 🐛 11 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2023-01-29 - A library for managing complex workflows.
* [SwiftRouter](https://github.com/skyline75489/SwiftRouter) ⚠️ Archived - A URL Router for iOS.
* [Marshroute](https://github.com/avito-tech/Marshroute) ⭐ 232 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2024-03-15 - Marshroute is an iOS Library for making your Routers simple but extremely powerful.
* [Linker](https://github.com/MaksimKurpa/Linker) ⭐ 139 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2018-05-04 - Lightweight way to handle internal and external deeplinks for iOS.

## Architecture Patterns

*Clean architecture, Viper, MVVM, Reactive... choose your weapon.*

* [The Composable Architecture](https://github.com/pointfreeco/swift-composable-architecture) ⭐ 14,875 | 🐛 26 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-07-24 - The Composable Architecture is a library for building applications in a consistent and understandable way, with composition, testing, and ergonomics in mind.
* [Clean Architecture for SwiftUI + Combine](https://github.com/nalexn/clean-architecture-swiftui) ⭐ 6,590 | 🐛 20 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2025-07-14 - A demo project showcasing the production setup of the SwiftUI app with Clean Architecture.
* [CleanArchitectureRxSwift](https://github.com/sergdort/CleanArchitectureRxSwift) ⭐ 4,103 | 🐛 2 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2025-02-12 - Example of Clean Architecture of iOS app using RxSwift.
* [ios-architecture](https://github.com/tailec/ios-architecture) ⭐ 1,524 | 🐛 4 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2022-05-16 - A collection of iOS architectures - MVC, MVVM, MVVM+RxSwift, VIPER, RIBs and many others.
* [iOS-Viper-Architecture](https://github.com/MindorksOpenSource/iOS-Viper-Architecture) ⭐ 736 | 🐛 4 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2024-07-20 - This repository contains a detailed sample app that implements VIPER architecture in iOS using libraries and frameworks like Alamofire, AlamofireImage, PKHUD, CoreData etc.
* [Viperit](https://github.com/ferranabello/Viperit) ⭐ 514 | 🐛 5 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2021-09-15 - Viper Framework for iOS. Develop an app following VIPER architecture in an easy way. Written and tested in Swift.
* [Reactant](https://github.com/Brightify/Reactant) ⭐ 370 | 🐛 8 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2021-11-09 - Reactant is a reactive architecture for iOS.
* [Spin](https://github.com/Spinners/Spin.Swift) ⭐ 124 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2021-01-16 - A universal implementation of a Feedback Loop system for RxSwift, ReactiveSwift and Combine
* [SwiftyVIPER](https://github.com/codytwinton/SwiftyVIPER) ⭐ 123 | 🐛 6 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2019-12-16 - Makes implementing VIPER architecture much easier and cleaner.

## Blockchain

*Tool for smart contract interactions. Bitcoin protocol implementations and Frameworks for interacting with cryptocurrencies.*

* [web3swift](https://github.com/web3swift-team/web3swift) ⭐ 890 | 🐛 74 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2025-09-24 - Elegant Web3js functionality in Swift. Native ABI parsing and smart contract interactions.
* [BitcoinKit](https://github.com/yenom/BitcoinKit) ⭐ 866 | 🐛 115 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2024-06-04 - Bitcoin protocol toolkit for Swift, BitcoinKit implements Bitcoin protocol in Swift. It is an implementation of the Bitcoin SPV protocol written in swift.
* [Web3.swift](https://github.com/Boilertalk/Web3.swift) ⭐ 694 | 🐛 59 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2024-08-14 - Web3 library for interacting with the Ethereum blockchain.
* [EthereumKit](https://github.com/yuzushioh/EthereumKit) ⭐ 484 | 🐛 13 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2021-12-07 - EthereumKit is a free, open-source Swift framework for easily interacting with the Ethereum.

## Cache

*Thread safe, offline and high performance cache libs and frameworks.*

* [HanekeSwift](https://github.com/Haneke/HanekeSwift) ⭐ 5,156 | 🐛 96 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2020-11-15 - A lightweight generic cache for iOS written in Swift with extra love for images.
* [Cache](https://github.com/hyperoslo/Cache) ⭐ 3,142 | 🐛 12 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2025-08-30 - Nothing but Cache.
* [Disk](https://github.com/saoudrizwan/Disk) ⭐ 3,113 | 🐛 14 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2024-08-08 - Delightful framework for iOS to easily persist structs, images, and data.
* [PINCache](https://github.com/pinterest/PINCache) ⭐ 2,691 | 🐛 30 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2026-08-14 - Fast, non-deadlocking parallel object cache for iOS and macOS.
* [YYCache](https://github.com/ibireme/YYCache) ⭐ 2,393 | 🐛 85 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2023-01-26 - High performance cache framework for iOS.
* [Awesome Cache](https://github.com/aschuch/AwesomeCache) ⭐ 1,266 | 🐛 36 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2021-08-11 - Delightful on-disk cache (written in Swift).
* [SPTPersistentCache](https://github.com/spotify/SPTPersistentCache) ⭐ 1,247 | 🐛 20 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2026-07-15 - Everyone tries to implement a cache at some point in their iOS app’s lifecycle, and this is ours. By Spotify.
* [RocketData](https://github.com/plivesey/RocketData) ⭐ 647 | 🐛 13 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2020-01-16 - A caching and consistency solution for immutable models.
* [mattress](https://github.com/buzzfeed/mattress) ⭐ 519 | 🐛 4 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2017-08-19 - iOS Offline Caching for Web Content.
* [Track](https://github.com/maquannene/Track) ⭐ 268 | 🐛 5 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2019-09-03 - Track is a thread safe cache write by Swift. Composed of DiskCache and MemoryCache which support LRU.

## Charts

*Explore beautiful, easy-to-use, and customizable iOS charting libraries, perfect for creating dynamic and stunning data visualizations.*

* [Charts](https://github.com/danielgindi/Charts) ⭐ 28,009 | 🐛 977 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-03-07 - A powerful chart/graph framework, the iOS equivalent to [MPAndroidChart](https://github.com/PhilJay/MPAndroidChart) ⭐ 38,194 | 🐛 2,191 | 🌐 Java | 📅 2025-06-05.
* [PNChart](https://github.com/kevinzhow/PNChart) ⭐ 9,645 | 🐛 173 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2018-07-02 - A simple and beautiful chart lib used in Piner and CoinsMan for iOS.
* [Scrollable-GraphView](https://github.com/philackm/ScrollableGraphView) ⭐ 5,287 | 🐛 67 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2020-12-02 - An adaptive scrollable graph view for iOS to visualise simple discrete datasets. Written in Swift.
* [JBChartView](https://github.com/Jawbone/JBChartView) ⭐ 3,701 | 🐛 10 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2018-08-06 - iOS-based charting library for both line and bar graphs.
* [core-plot](https://github.com/core-plot/core-plot) ⭐ 2,762 | 🐛 47 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2026-05-04 - A 2D plotting lib which is highly customizable and capable of drawing many types of plots.
* [TEAChart](https://github.com/xhacker/TEAChart) ⭐ 1,189 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2017-11-17 - Simple and intuitive iOS chart library. Contribution graph, clock chart, and bar chart.
* [SwiftChart](https://github.com/gpbl/SwiftChart) ⭐ 1,047 | 🐛 44 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2022-04-24 - Line and area chart library for iOS.
* [FSLineChart](https://github.com/ArthurGuibert/FSLineChart) ⭐ 844 | 🐛 15 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2017-08-09 - A line chart library for iOS.
* [EatFit](https://github.com/Yalantis/EatFit) ⭐ 648 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2020-04-13 - Eat fit is a component for attractive data representation inspired by Google Fit.
* [EChart](https://github.com/zhuhuihuihui/EChart) ⭐ 644 | 🐛 15 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2016-08-08 - iOS/iPhone/iPad Chart, Graph. Event handling and animation supported.
* [FSInteractiveMap](https://github.com/ArthurGuibert/FSInteractiveMap) ⭐ 546 | 🐛 18 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2018-10-18 - A charting library to visualize and interact with a vector map on iOS. It's like Geochart but for iOS.
* [MagicPie](https://github.com/AlexandrGraschenkov/MagicPie) ⭐ 526 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2024-06-12 - Awesome layer-based pie chart. Fantastically fast and fully customizable. Amazing animations available with MagicPie.
* [PieCharts](https://github.com/i-schuetz/PieCharts) ⭐ 508 | 🐛 27 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2023-03-23 - Easy to use and highly customizable pie charts library for iOS.
* [ANDLineChartView](https://github.com/anaglik/ANDLineChartView) ⭐ 420 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2017-03-26 - ANDLineChartView is easy to use view-based class for displaying animated line chart.

* [AwesomeiOS Weekly](http://weekly.awesomeios.com) - AwesomeiOS Weekly.

* [Indie Watch](https://indie.watch/) - A weekly newsletter featuring the best apps made by indie iOS developers.
* [iOS Cookies Newsletter](https://us11.campaign-archive.com/home/?u=cd1f3ed33c6527331d82107ba\&id=532dc7fb64) - A weekly digest of new iOS libraries written in Swift.
* [iOS Dev Tools Weekly](https://iosdev.tools) - The greatest iOS development tools, including websites, desktop and mobile apps, and back-end services.
* [iOS Dev Weekly](https://iosdevweekly.com/) - Subscribe to a hand-picked round up of the best iOS development links every week. Free.
* [iOS Goodies](https://ios-goodies.com) - Weekly iOS newsletter.
* [iOS Trivia Weekly](https://wanderbit.us4.list-manage.com/subscribe?u=4e20cd8ea3a0ce09ff4619a52\&id=5898a5992b) - Three challenging questions about iOS development every Wednesday.
* [Mobile Developers Cafe](https://mobiledeveloperscafe.com) - A weekly newsletter for Mobile developers with loads of iOS content.
* [raywenderlich.com Weekly](https://www.raywenderlich.com/newsletter) - sign up to receive the latest tutorials from raywenderlich.com each week.
* [Server-Side Swift Weekly](https://www.serverswift.tech) - A weekly newsletter with the best links related to server-side Swift and cross-platform developer tools. Curated by [@maxdesiatov](https://twitter.com/maxdesiatov)
* [Swift Developments](https://andybargh.com/swiftdevelopments/) - A weekly curated newsletter containing a hand picked selection of the latest links, videos, tools and tutorials for people interested in designing and developing their own iOS, WatchOS and AppleTV apps using Swift.
* [Swift Weekly Brief](https://swiftweekly.github.io/) - A community-driven weekly newsletter about Swift.org. Curated by Jesse Squires and published for free every Thursday.
* [SwiftLee](https://www.avanderlee.com/) - A weekly blog about Swift, iOS and Xcode Tips and Tricks.

## Xcode

*Extensions, themes and other enhancements for Xcode.*

### Extensions

*Xcode source editor and project extensions.*

* [XVim2](https://github.com/XVimProject/XVim2) ⭐ 2,286 | 🐛 98 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2023-01-14 - Vim key-bindings for Xcode 9.
* [Import](https://github.com/markohlebar/Import) ⭐ 838 | 🐛 10 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2022-04-14 - Xcode extension for adding imports from anywhere in the code.
* [SwiftInitializerGenerator](https://github.com/Bouke/SwiftInitializerGenerator) ⭐ 714 | 🐛 7 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2019-08-13 - Xcode 8 Source Code Extension to Generate Swift Initializers.
* [nef](https://github.com/bow-swift/nef-plugin) ⭐ 201 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2021-11-11 - This Xcode extension enables you to make a code selection and export it to a snippets. Available on Mac AppStore.
* [XcodeEquatableGenerator](https://github.com/sergdort/XcodeEquatableGenerator) ⭐ 188 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2019-03-27 - Xcode 8 Source Code Extension will generate conformance to Swift Equatable protocol based on type and fields selection.
* [CleanClosureXcode](https://github.com/BalestraPatrick/CleanClosureXcode) ⭐ 181 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2018-01-06 - An Xcode Source Editor extension to clean the closure syntax.
* [Comment Spell Checker](https://github.com/velyan/Comment-Spell-Checker) ⭐ 159 | 🐛 2 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2018-12-26 - Xcode extension for spell checking and auto correcting code comments.
* [Mark](https://github.com/velyan/Mark) ⭐ 157 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2019-03-30 - Xcode extension for generating MARK comments.
* [Snowonder](https://github.com/Karetski/Snowonder) ⭐ 107 | 🐛 4 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-05-20 - Magical import declarations formatter for Xcode.
* [XGist](https://github.com/Bunn/Xgist) ⭐ 81 | 🐛 4 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2017-05-27 - Xcode extension which allows you to send your text selection or entire file to GitHub's Gist and automatically copy the Gist URL into your Clipboard.
* [XShared](https://github.com/Otbivnoe/XShared) ⚠️ Archived - Xcode extension which allows you copying the code with special formatting quotes for social (Slack, Telegram).
* [DocumenterXcode](https://github.com/serhii-londar/DocumenterXcode) ⭐ 35 | 🐛 3 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2018-10-12 - Attempt to give a new life for VVDocumenter-Xcode as source editor extension.
* [xTextHandler](https://github.com/cyanzhong/xTextHandler) - Xcode Source Editor Extension Toolset (Plugins for Xcode 8).
* [Swiftify](https://swiftify.com/) - Objective-C to Swift online code converter and Xcode extension.

### Other Xcode

*Additional Xcode plugins, helpers and utilities.*

* [Synx](https://github.com/venmo/synx) ⭐ 6,043 | 🐛 56 | 🌐 Ruby | 📅 2019-07-18 - A command-line tool that reorganizes your Xcode project folder to match your Xcode groups.
* [Swift Macros 🚀](https://github.com/krzysztofzablocki/Swift-Macros) ⭐ 2,468 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-02-15 - A curated list of community-created Macros and associated learning resources.
* [SBShortcutMenuSimulator](https://github.com/DeskConnect/SBShortcutMenuSimulator) ⭐ 1,732 | 🐛 13 | 🌐 Objective-C | 📅 2016-12-11 - 3D Touch shortcuts in the Simulator.
* [Xcode Developer Disk Images](https://github.com/haikieu/xcode-developer-disk-image-all-platforms) ⭐ 322 | 🐛 7 | 📅 2023-07-05 - Xcode Developer Disk Images is needed when you want to put your build to the device, however sometimes your Xcode is not updated with the latest Disk Images, you could find them here for convenience.
* [awesome-xcode-scripts](https://github.com/aashishtamsya/awesome-xcode-scripts) ⭐ 128 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Shell | 📅 2017-10-20 - A curated list of useful xcode scripts.
* [Xcode Keymap for Visual Studio Code](https://marketplace.visualstudio.com/items?itemName=stevemoser.xcode-keybindings) - This extension ports popular Xcode keyboard shortcuts to Visual Studio Code.
